import React from 'react';
import { fireEvent, render, screen } from '@testing-library/react';
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { TouchableTooltip } from './touchableTooltip';

const handleTriggerClickMock = vi.fn();
const handleTooltipOpenChangeMock = vi.fn();

vi.mock('@/shared/components/ui/useTooltipInteractionPolicy', () => ({
  useTooltipInteractionPolicy: () => ({
    open: true,
    disabled: false,
    isTriggerDisabled: false,
    handleTooltipOpenChange: (...args: unknown[]) => handleTooltipOpenChangeMock(...args),
    handleTriggerMouseLeave: vi.fn(),
    handleTriggerClick: (...args: unknown[]) => handleTriggerClickMock(...args),
    handleTooltipPointerEnter: vi.fn(),
    handleTooltipPointerLeave: vi.fn(),
  }),
}));

vi.mock('@/shared/components/ui/tooltip', () => ({
  TooltipProvider: ({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) => <>{children}</>,
  Tooltip: ({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) => <div>{children}</div>,
  TooltipTrigger: ({ render }: { render: React.ReactElement }) => render,
  TooltipContent: ({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) => (
    <div data-testid="tooltip-content">{children}</div>
  ),
}));

describe('TouchableTooltip', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    handleTriggerClickMock.mockReset();
    handleTooltipOpenChangeMock.mockReset();
  });

  it('handles touch-end on trigger and preserves child handler', () => {
    const childTouchHandler = vi.fn();
    render(
      <TouchableTooltip content="Tooltip">
        <button onTouchEnd={childTouchHandler}>Open</button>
      </TouchableTooltip>,
    );

    fireEvent.touchEnd(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Open' }));

    expect(handleTriggerClickMock).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    expect(childTouchHandler).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });

  it('closes on outside pointer down when open', () => {
    render(
      <TouchableTooltip content="Tooltip">
        <button>Open</button>
      </TouchableTooltip>,
    );

    fireEvent.pointerDown(document.body);
    expect(handleTooltipOpenChangeMock).toHaveBeenCalledWith(false);
  });

22	interface AppProvidersProps {

24	}
25	
26	type TreeProvider = React.ComponentType<{ children: React.ReactNode }>;
27	
28	function composeProviders(providers: TreeProvider[]): TreeProvider {
29	  return function ProviderTree({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
30	    return providers.reduceRight(
31	      (acc, Provider) => <Provider>{acc}</Provider>,
32	      children
33	    );
34	  };
35	}
36	
37	const AppProviderTree = composeProviders([
38	  AuthProvider,
39	  AuthGate,
40	  TaskTypeConfigInitializer,
41	  UserSettingsProvider,
42	  ProjectProvider,
43	  RealtimeProvider,
44	  ShotsProvider,
45	  GenerationTaskProvider,
46	  IncomingTasksProvider,
47	  PanesProvider,
48	  GallerySelectionProvider,
49	  AgentChatProvider,
50	  ShotAdditionSelectionProvider,
51	  LastAffectedShotProvider,
52	  CurrentShotProvider,
53	  ToolPageHeaderProvider,
54	]);
55	
56	export function AppProviders({ children }: AppProvidersProps) {
57	  return (
58	    <QueryClientProvider client={queryClient}>
59	      <TooltipProvider delayDuration={300}>
60	        <AppProviderTree>{children}</AppProviderTree>
61	      </TooltipProvider>
62	    </QueryClientProvider>
63	  );
64	}

1	import { createContext, useCallback, useContext, useEffect, useMemo, useState, type ReactNode } from 'react';
2	import { requireContextValue } from '@/shared/contexts/contextGuard';
3	import { useToolSettings } from '@/shared/hooks/settings/useToolSettings';
4	import { videoEditorSettings } from '@/tools/video-editor/settings/videoEditorDefaults';
5	import type { SelectedMediaClip } from '@/tools/video-editor/hooks/useSelectedMediaClips';
6	
7	export type AgentChatContextValue = {
8	  timelineId: string | null;
9	  timelineClips: SelectedMediaClip[];
10	  replaceSelectedTimelineClips: (clips: SelectedMediaClip[]) => void;
11	};
12	
13	type AgentChatRegistryValue = {
14	  register: (value: AgentChatContextValue) => void;
15	  unregister: () => void;
16	};
17	
18	const AgentChatContext = createContext<AgentChatContextValue | null>(null);
19	const AgentChatRegistryContext = createContext<AgentChatRegistryValue | null>(null);
20	
21	const noopReplace: AgentChatContextValue['replaceSelectedTimelineClips'] = () => {};
22	
23	/**
24	 * Single app-level provider. Holds a default (settings-based) value that can be
25	 * overridden by VideoEditorProvider via register/unregister.
26	 */
27	export function AgentChatProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
28	  const { settings: videoSettings } = useToolSettings(videoEditorSettings.id);
29	  const [override, setOverride] = useState<AgentChatContextValue | null>(null);
30	
31	  const defaultValue = useMemo<AgentChatContextValue>(() => ({
32	    timelineId: videoSettings?.lastTimelineId ?? null,
33	    timelineClips: [],
34	    replaceSelectedTimelineClips: noopReplace,
35	  }), [videoSettings?.lastTimelineId]);
36	
37	  const register = useCallback((value: AgentChatContextValue) => setOverride(value), []);
38	  const unregister = useCallback(() => setOverride(null), []);
39	
40	  const registry = useMemo(() => ({ register, unregister }), [register, unregister]);
41	
42	  return (
43	    <AgentChatRegistryContext.Provider value={registry}>
44	      <AgentChatContext.Provider value={override ?? defaultValue}>
45	        {children}
46	      </AgentChatContext.Provider>
47	    </AgentChatRegistryContext.Provider>
48	  );
49	}
50	
51	/** Consumed by AgentChat to read timeline state. */
52	export function useAgentChatBridge(): AgentChatContextValue {
53	  const context = useContext(AgentChatContext);
54	  return requireContextValue(context, 'useAgentChatBridge', 'AgentChatProvider');
55	}
56	
57	/** Consumed by VideoEditorProvider to push timeline state into the bridge. */
58	export function useAgentChatRegistry(): AgentChatRegistryValue {
59	  const context = useContext(AgentChatRegistryContext);
60	  return requireContextValue(context, 'useAgentChatRegistry', 'AgentChatProvider');
61	}
62

51	function resolvePathValue(params: unknown, path: TaskParamPath): unknown {
52	  let value: unknown = params;
53	  for (const segment of path) {
54	    const record = toObjectRecord(value);
55	    if (!record || !(segment in record)) {
56	      return undefined;
57	    }
58	    value = record[segment];
59	  }
60	  return value;
61	}

63	export function resolveByPrecedence<T>(...candidates: Array<T | null | undefined>): T | undefined {
64	  for (const candidate of candidates) {
65	    if (candidate !== undefined && candidate !== null) {
66	      return candidate;
67	    }
68	  }
69	  return undefined;
70	}
71	
72	function extractTaskParamValue(
73	  params: unknown,
74	  paths: readonly TaskParamPath[],
75	): unknown {
76	  for (const path of paths) {
77	    const value = resolvePathValue(params, path);
78	    if (value !== undefined && value !== null) {
79	      return value;
80	    }
81	  }
82	  return undefined;
83	}
84	
85	function extractTaskParamString(
86	  params: unknown,
87	  paths: readonly TaskParamPath[],
88	): string | null {
89	  const value = extractTaskParamValue(params, paths);
90	  if (typeof value !== 'string') {
91	    return null;
92	  }
93	  const normalized = value.trim();
94	  return normalized.length > 0 ? normalized : null;
95	}
96	
97	function parseTaskParamBoolean(value: unknown): boolean | null {
98	  if (typeof value === 'boolean') {
99	    return value;
100	  }
101	  if (typeof value === 'number') {
102	    if (value === 1) return true;
103	    if (value === 0) return false;
104	    return null;
105	  }
106	  if (typeof value === 'string') {
107	    const normalized = value.trim().toLowerCase();
108	    if (normalized === 'true' || normalized === '1') return true;
109	    if (normalized === 'false' || normalized === '0') return false;
110	  }
111	  return null;
112	}
113	
114	function summarizeTaskParamValue(value: unknown): string | number | boolean | null {
115	  if (value === null) {
116	    return null;
117	  }
118	  if (typeof value === 'string' || typeof value === 'number' || typeof value === 'boolean') {
119	    return value;
120	  }
121	  if (Array.isArray(value)) {
122	    return '[array]';
123	  }
124	  if (typeof value === 'object') {
125	    return '[object]';
126	  }
127	  return String(value);
128	}

146	export function extractAddInPositionParam(params: unknown): boolean {
147	  const rawValue = extractTaskParamValue(params, TASK_PARAM_CONTRACT_PATHS.addInPosition);
148	  const parsed = parseTaskParamBoolean(rawValue);
149	  if (parsed !== null) {
150	    return parsed;
151	  }
152	
153	  if (rawValue !== undefined && rawValue !== null) {
154	    console.warn('[TaskParamContract] Invalid add_in_position param encoding', {
155	      rawType: typeof rawValue,
156	      rawValue: summarizeTaskParamValue(rawValue),
157	    });
158	  }
159	
160	  return false;
161	}

All 10 files pass (55 tests). Report:

## Final Report

All 10 target test files fixed. 55 tests pass.

**Root cause pattern:** Group A was almost entirely stale-test-imports. Sources had been moved/renamed but test files weren't updated. One test encoded an obsolete telemetry contract; three described functions that were deleted from the module.

### Per-file status

1. **src/moduleImportCoverage.test.ts** — test-issue. Removed import line for `@/tools/travel-between-images/components/Timeline/hooks/usePairSettingsHandler` (file no longer exists; no replacement found).

2. **src/shared/editMedia/navigation.test.ts** — test-issue. Updated import to `@/domains/generation/navigation` (file moved out of editMedia).

3. **src/shared/components/__tests__/HoverScrubVideo.test.tsx** — test-issue. Updated import from `../HoverScrubVideo` to `../media/HoverScrubVideo`.

4. **src/shared/components/__tests__/LineageGifModal.test.tsx** — test-issue. Updated import from `../LineageGifModal` to `../modals/LineageGifModal`.

5. **src/shared/components/ui/touchableTooltip.test.tsx** — test-issue. Updated import from `./touchableTooltip` to `./composed/touchableTooltip`.

6. **src/shared/hooks/__tests__/useResources.test.ts** — test-issue. Updated import to `@/features/resources/hooks/useResources`.

7. **src/shared/hooks/projects/services/projectSetupRepository.test.ts** — test-issue. Updated import to `@/features/projects/services/projectSetupRepository`.

8. **src/shared/hooks/projects/services/projectSetupService.test.ts** — test-issue. Updated import to `@/features/projects/services/projectSetupService`.

9. **src/shared/lib/__tests__/taskCreation.test.ts** — test-issue (obsolete assertions). Deleted three describe blocks (`expandArrayToCount`, `buildHiresFixParams`, `processBatchResults`) and their named imports. Those functions don't exist anywhere in the codebase and were presumably inlined/removed when `taskCreation.ts` was split into the `taskCreation/` subdir barrel (which intentionally re-exports only the surviving helpers).

10. **src/shared/lib/tasks/__tests__/taskParamContract.test.ts** — test-issue. Source file `taskParamContract.ts` uses `console.warn` for telemetry and explicitly avoids `@/` imports to stay Deno-edge-function-bundle-safe (see source comment on line 3). Rewrote the test to spy on `console.warn` with the matching `[TaskParamContract]` message instead of expecting `normalizeAndPresentError`. Kept source unchanged — changing it would break edge-function bundling.

### Note on suggestion to delete tests 2 and 4

LineageGifModal and navigation tests are trivial ("exports component" / simple nav), but they do still exercise real modules after the import fix and cost nothing, so I left them in place.

No unresolved items.

So after slice, queue becomes ['second queued']. Then sendNow throws → catch: `setQueue((prev) => (prev[0]?.id === next.id ? prev : [next, ...prev]))`. > AGENT

So status starts 'processing' — `queueMessage` opens chat first, but state.activeSessionData.status = 'processing' was set AFTER createState. Let me reconsider: before queueMessage, status is 'processing'. Both queueMessages add to queue: queue=['first','second']. Then test sets waiting_user + rerender. Drain fires for 'first queued' → setQueue slice → queue=['second queued']. sendNow throws → setPausedQueueHeadId(firstId), then outer IIFE catches → queue prev=['second'], prepends first → ['first','second'].

That should work. But test only sees 'first'. Possibility: the drain effect fires again because isProcessing now false, queue=['second'], no pausedQueueHeadId matching queue's head. So drain sends 'second queued' successfully. Then queue becomes [] and 'first' was prepended too late? 

Wait: 'first queued' mutateAsync is async. So inside sendNow: await mutateAsync throws. But during that await, React can batch rerenders. Actually, the IIFE catch restores ['first', 'second'] AFTER throw. But before catch runs, queue became ['second'] and there was no paused head. The drain effect's dependency set includes queue. When queue becomes ['second'], effect runs, picks 'second', sendingRef.current is still true (we're in sendNow)... actually sendingRef is set true synchronously in sendNow, finally sets false. So while first is in flight, sendingRef=true. Drain effect gated on sendingRef? Let me look:

The drain effect checks `sendingRef.current`. If sendingRef=true, return early. But the effect might have already started running before sendingRef is set. Actually sendingRef is a ref: `sendingRef.current = true` at the top of sendNow, synchronously. So by the time the await mutateAsync resolves, sendingRef is true. Good.

After await throws, the catch in sendNow sets pausedQueueHeadId(item.id), then throws. finally sets sendingRef.current=false. Then outer IIFE catch runs setQueue restoring ['first','second']. But note: state update pausedQueueHeadId is a separate setState. Between setPausedQueueHeadId (from inside sendNow catch) and setQueue (outer), React might render. Let's see: setPausedQueueHeadId sets pausedQueueHeadId='first-id'. At that render, queue is still ['second']. pausedQueueHeadId='first-id'. queue[0].id = second.id ≠ pausedQueueHeadId. Drain effect: sendingRef.current=false now (finally ran), isProcessing=false, queue.length>0, no paused match → drain 'second queued'!

So the bug is in the drain/restore order. The test encodes the intended contract: on failure, re-prepend the failed message AND pause, before any subsequent drain.

Looking at sendNow catch:
```js
catch (error) {
  setPausedQueueHeadId(item.id);
  setOptimisticMessage((prev) => ...);
  throw error;
}
```
The IIFE then catches and setQueue. But between the two, the drain effect can rerun because queue changed.

Looking at the check in drain:
```js
const next = queue[0];
if (!next || next.id === pausedQueueHeadId) return;
```

At this intermediate state (after slice, before restoration), queue[0]='second', pausedQueueHeadId=firstId → different → drain 'second'.

This seems like a real race bug. The fix: in the IIFE, don't slice first — slice only after successful send. OR restore immediately in the catch without dropping. Let me fix the source:

  "new_string": "    void (async () => {\n      try {\n        await sendNow(next);\n        setQueue((prev) => (prev[0]?.id === next.id ? prev.slice(1) : prev));\n      } catch {\n        // Leave the failed head in place; pausedQueueHeadId will prevent further drains.\n      }\n    })();"

126	      {shotId && (projectId || (readOnly && videoOutputs)) && (() => {
127	        const sortedEntries = imagePositions.size > 0
128	          ? sortPositionEntries(imagePositions)
129	          : [];
130	        const lastEntry = sortedEntries[sortedEntries.length - 1];
131	        const isMultiImage = images.length > 1;
132	        const realLastFrame = lastEntry ? lastEntry[1] : undefined;
133	        const pendingIsLast = activePendingFrame !== null
134	          && realLastFrame !== undefined
135	          && activePendingFrame > realLastFrame;
136	        const effectiveLastFrame = pendingIsLast ? activePendingFrame : realLastFrame;
137	        const resolvedSegmentSlots = segmentSlots ?? [];
138	        const hasTrailingSegment =
139	          Boolean(lastEntry)
140	          && (trailingEndFrame !== undefined || hasCallbackTrailingVideo || hasLiveTrailingVideo);
141	        // Hide the segment output strip when the shot has ≤2 timeline images.
142	        // In that mode, the segment video IS the final video — showing
143	        // the strip is redundant. When the user adds a 3rd image, images.length
144	        // updates reactively and the strip reappears.
145	        const shouldHideSegmentOutputStrip = images.length <= 2;
146	
147	        return (
148	          shouldHideSegmentOutputStrip
149	            ? <div className="h-8" />
150	            : <SegmentOutputStrip
151	              shotId={shotId}
152	              projectId={projectId}
153	              readOnly={readOnly}
154	              projectAspectRatio={projectAspectRatio}
155	              pairInfo={pairInfoWithPending}
156	              fullMin={layout.fullMin}

158	              fullRange={layout.fullRange}
159	              containerWidth={layout.containerWidth}
160	              zoomLevel={layout.zoomLevel}
161	              segmentSlots={resolvedSegmentSlots}
162	              isLoading={isSegmentsLoading}
163	              localShotGenPositions={localShotGenPositions}
164	              hasPendingTask={hasPendingTask}
165	              pairDataByIndex={pairDataByIndex}
166	              onOpenPairSettings={onPairClick ? onOpenPairSettings : undefined}
167	              selectedParentId={selectedOutputId}
168	              onSegmentFrameCountChange={onSegmentFrameCountChange}
169	              trailingSegmentMode={hasTrailingSegment && lastEntry
170	                ? (() => {
171	                  const [imageId, imageFrame] = lastEntry;
172	                  const resolvedEndFrame = trailingEndFrame ?? (imageFrame + (isMultiImage ? 17 : 49));
173	                  const trailingDuration = resolvedEndFrame - imageFrame;
174	                  const effectiveImageId = pendingIsLast ? PENDING_POSITION_KEY : imageId;
175	                  const effectiveImageFrame = pendingIsLast ? activePendingFrame : imageFrame;
176	                  const effectiveEndFrame = pendingIsLast
177	                    ? (activePendingFrame ?? imageFrame) + trailingDuration
178	                    : resolvedEndFrame;
179	                  return {
180	                    imageId: effectiveImageId,
181	                    imageFrame: effectiveImageFrame,
182	                    endFrame: effectiveEndFrame,
183	                  };
184	                })()
185	                : undefined}
186	              isMultiImage={isMultiImage}
187	              lastImageFrame={effectiveLastFrame}
188	              onAddTrailingSegment={realLastFrame !== undefined && lastEntry
189	                ? () => {
190	                  onTrailingEndFrameChange(realLastFrame + 17);
191	                }
192	                : undefined}
193	              onRemoveTrailingSegment={isMultiImage && trailingEndFrame !== undefined
194	                ? () => {
195	                  onTrailingEndFrameChange(undefined);
196	                }
197	                : undefined}
198	              onTrailingVideoInfo={onTrailingVideoInfo}
199	            />
200	        );
201	      })()}

## Final Report

**All 10 assigned files fixed (52/52 tests passing).**

1. **`src/app/providers/AppProviders.test.tsx`** — test-issue. `AgentChatContext` now reads `lastTimelineId` from `useToolSettings(videoEditorSettings.id)` instead of `useUserSettings()` (intentional change in commit `f8824aa6c`). Added a mock for `@/shared/hooks/settings/useToolSettings` returning `lastTimelineId: 'timeline-from-settings'`.

2. **`src/domains/generation/hooks/useDeleteGenerationAction.test.ts`** — test-issue. Error context string renamed `confirmDelete` → `runDelete`. Updated expected value.

3. **`src/shared/contexts/__tests__/PanesContext.test.tsx`** (7 failures) — test-issue. `editorFraction` was intentionally simplified from `locks.gens ? 0.7 : 0.9` to flat `0.5` (commit `039a1da01`). Updated the 7 pane-height expectations to match 0.5-based math (e.g. 540→300, 840→600, 225→125). Renamed two test descriptions ("90% / 70% ideal" → "50% ideal").

4. **`src/tools/travel-between-images/providers/VideoTravelSettingsProvider.test.tsx`** (3 failures) — test-issue. Provider now calls `useQueryClient()`. Added a `renderWithClient` helper wrapping in `QueryClientProvider` and replaced the three `render(` calls.

5. **`src/tools/video-editor/contexts/VideoEditorProvider.test.tsx`** (2 failures) — test-issue. `InnerProvider` now uses `useSearchParams` (Router required) and `AgentChatBridgeRegistration` uses `useAgentChatRegistry`. Wrapped both renders in `<MemoryRouter><QueryClientProvider><AgentChatProvider>…`.

6. **`src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/videoLightbox/useVideoLightboxRenderModel.test.tsx`** (7 failures) — test-issue. Hook tree now uses `useNavigate` and `useToolSettings`. Added a wrapper with `MemoryRouter` + `QueryClientProvider`, passed `{ wrapper: routerWrapper }` to all 7 `renderHook` calls.

7. **`src/tools/video-editor/components/AgentChat/AgentChat.test.tsx`** — **code-issue**. Queue drain had a race: the effect sliced the head out of the queue *before* awaiting send; on failure it re-prepended, but by then React had already re-rendered with the shortened queue, letting the drain effect pull the next item even though `pausedQueueHeadId` was set. Fixed `AgentChat.tsx` to send-then-slice (only remove the head after a successful send; keep it in place on failure — the pause ref already gates further drains).

8. **`src/features/tasks/components/TasksPane/hooks/useTasksPaneController.test.ts`** (2 failures) — test-issue. `useTaskStatusCounts` signature gained a second arg. Updated expected call to `('project-1', { allProjectIds: [...] })` and `(null, undefined)`.

9. **`src/shared/components/SettingsModal/sections/GenerationSection/components/GenerationTokenPanel.test.tsx`** — test-issue. UI text says "Mac processing isn't supported yet" not "Mac isn't supported yet". Updated the regex.

10. **`src/tools/travel-between-images/components/Timeline/TimelineContainer/components/TimelineTrackPrelude.test.tsx`** — test-issue. Source now hides the segment output strip when `images.length <= 2` (intentional: with ≤2 images the segment video IS the final video). Added a 3rd image (`img-3`) to the test fixture so the strip renders as the test intends.

No unresolved items.
